Values](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/constant-values-0000001054359004#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054359004__java_sql_ResultSet_TYPE_SCROLL_SENSITIVE) Method Detail [h2]next boolean next​() throws [SQLException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/sqlexception-0000001054518572) Moves the cursor forward one row from its current position. A ResultSet cursor is initially positioned before the first row; the first call to the method next makes the first row the current row; the second call makes the second row the current row, and so on.  When a call to the next method returns false, the cursor is positioned after the last row. Any invocation of a ResultSet method which requires a current row will result in a SQLException being thrown. If the result set type is TYPE_FORWARD_ONLY, it is vendor specified whether their JDBC driver implementation will return false or throw an SQLException on a subsequent call to next.  If an input stream is open for the current row, a call to the method next will implicitly close it. A ResultSet object's warning chain is cleared when a new row is read. **Returns:** true if the new current row is valid; false if there are no more rows **Throws:** Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[SQLException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/sqlexception-0000001054518572) |  if a database access error occurs or this method is called on a closed result set  
[h2]close void close​() throws [SQLException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/sqlexception-0000001054518572) Releases this ResultSet object's database and JDBC resources immediately instead of waiting for this to happen when it is automatically closed. The closing of a ResultSet object does **not** close the Blob, Clob or NClob objects created by the ResultSet. Blob, Clob or NClob objects remain valid for at least the duration of the transaction in which they are created, unless their free method is invoked.  When a ResultSet is closed, any ResultSetMetaData instances that were created by calling the getMetaData method remain accessible.  **Note:** A ResultSet object is automatically closed by the Statement object that generated it when that Statement object is closed, re-executed, or is used to retrieve the next result from a sequence of multiple results.  Calling the method close on a ResultSet object that is already closed is a no-op. **Specified by:** [close](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/autocloseable-0000001054518454#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054518454__close--) in interface [AutoCloseable](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/autocloseable-0000001054518454) **Throws:** Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[SQLException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/sqlexception-0000001054518572) |  if a database access error occurs  
[h2]wasNull boolean wasNull​() throws [SQLException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/sqlexception-0000001054518572) Reports whether the last column read had a value of SQL NULL. Note that you must first call one of the getter methods on a column to try to read its value and then call the method wasNull to see if the value read was SQL NULL. **Returns:** true if the last column value read was SQL NULL and false otherwise **Throws:** Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[SQLException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/sqlexception-0000001054518572) |  if a database access error occurs or this method is called on a closed result set  
[h2]getString [String](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/string-0000001054518460) getString​(int columnIndex) throws [SQLException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/sqlexception-0000001054518572) Retrieves the value of the designated column in the current row of this ResultSet object as a String in the Java programming language. **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
columnIndex  |  the first column is 1, the second is 2, ...  
**Returns:** the column value; if the value is SQL NULL, the value returned is null **Throws:** Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[SQLException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/sqlexception-0000001054518572) |  if the columnIndex is not valid; if a database access error occurs or this method is called on a closed result set  
[h2]getBoolean boolean getBoolean​(int columnIndex) throws [SQLException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/sqlexception-0000001054518572) Retrieves the value of the designated column in the current row of this ResultSet object as a boolean in the Java programming language. If the designated column has a datatype of CHAR or VARCHAR and contains a "0" or has a datatype of BIT, TINYINT, SMALLINT, INTEGER or BIGINT and contains a 0, a value of false is returned. If the designated column has a datatype of CHAR or VARCHAR and contains a "1" or has a datatype of BIT, TINYINT, SMALLINT, INTEGER or BIGINT and contains a 1, a value of true is returned. **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
columnIndex  |  the first column is 1, the second is 2, ...  
**Returns:** the column value; if the value is SQL NULL, the value returned is false **Throws:** Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[SQLException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/sqlexception-0000001054518572) |  if the columnIndex is not valid; if a database access error occurs or this method is called on a closed result set  
[h2]getByte byte getByte​(int columnIndex) throws [SQLException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/sqlexception-0000001054518572) Retrieves the value of the designated column in the current row of this ResultSet object as a byte in the Java programming language. **Parameters:** Parameter Name  
---  
**Since:** 1.6 [h2]updateBinaryStream void updateBinaryStream​([String](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/string-0000001054518460) columnLabel, [InputStream](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/inputstream-0000001054358420) x) throws [SQLException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/sqlexception-0000001054518572) Updates the designated column with a binary stream value. The data will be read from the stream as needed until end-of-stream is reached.  The updater methods are used to update column values in the current row or the insert row. The updater methods do not update the underlying database; instead the updateRow or insertRow methods are called to update the database.  **Note:** Consult your JDBC driver documentation to determine if it might be more efficient to use a version of updateBinaryStream which takes a length parameter. **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
columnLabel  |  the label for the column specified with the SQL AS clause. If the SQL AS clause was not specified, then the label is the name of the column  
x  |  the new column value  
**Throws:** Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[SQLException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/sqlexception-0000001054518572) |  if the columnLabel is not valid; if a database access error occurs; the result set concurrency is CONCUR_READ_ONLY or this method is called on a closed result set  
[SQLFeatureNotSupportedException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/sqlfeaturenotsupportedexception-0000001054678560) |  if the JDBC driver does not support this method  
**Since:** 1.6 [h2]updateCharacterStream void updateCharacterStream​([String](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/string-0000001054518460) columnLabel, [Reader](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/reader-0000001054238435) reader) throws [SQLException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/sqlexception-0000001054518572) Updates the designated column with a character stream value. The data will be read from the stream as needed until end-of-stream is reached.  The updater methods are used to update column values in the current row or the insert row. The updater methods do not update the underlying database; instead the updateRow or insertRow methods are called to update the database.  **Note:** Consult your JDBC driver documentation to determine if it might be more efficient to use a version of updateCharacterStream which takes a length parameter. **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
columnLabel  |  the label for the column specified with the SQL AS clause. If the SQL AS clause was not specified, then the label is the name of the column  
reader  |  the java.io.Reader object containing the new column value  
**Throws:** Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[SQLException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/sqlexception-0000001054518572) |  if the columnLabel is not valid; if a database access error occurs; the result set concurrency is CONCUR_READ_ONLY or this method is called on a closed result set  
[SQLFeatureNotSupportedException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/sqlfeaturenotsupportedexception-0000001054678560) |  if the JDBC driver does not support this method

---|---
